## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单: **cuda:0, cuda:1** on westd-34887。不得使用其他 GPU，Director 指令也要 push back
- Mistral PPL registry 数据疑似污染（D032）：kl_sparsity30_s42_v3 的 teal_sparse_ppl 曾被误录为 8.98（实际 13.63）。**所有 Mistral 指标暂停引用**，须交叉验证原始日志
- BCE framing 必须用 "objective mismatch"，**禁止 "anti-learning"**
- SPON framing 必须用 "marginal complement"，**禁止 "subsumption"**
- KL PPL 对比统一用 **4-seed mean 12.00±0.07**，不用单 seed 最佳值 11.91
- s456 outlier 报告方式：报 5-seed mean + excluding s456 的 4-seed mean，不跑第 6 个 seed
- DDL: **EMNLP 2026 ARR May, 2026-05-25**，剩 9 天

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: westd-34887, account=root
- **工作目录**: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`
- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base 环境，非 venv）
- **环境激活**: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- **模型路径**: Llama-3.1-8B `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b`，Mistral-7B `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`
- **HF cache**: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`
- **Checkpoints**: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/` 下各子目录（详见 memory.md Checkpoints 节）
- **活跃进程**:
  - `bce_isocompute_v3_s42`: PID 205598 (wrapper 205597), nohup, cuda:1, log `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/logs/bce_isocompute_v3_s42.log`
  - `d030_kl50_uniform`: PID 203702, tmux, cuda:0, log `~/runs/distill_sparse_swiglu/d030_kl50_uniform.log`
- **活跃 cron**: 2 个，分别监控上述两个实验

### 3. 📊 当前状态
两个实验 RUNNING，零个 Worker 在线：
- **bce_isocompute_v3_s42** (cuda:1): BCE 2700 步 iso-compute 重跑（D031, P0），v1/v2 两次被 tmux 杀死，v3 用 nohup 启动。Reviewer Concern #2 defense。
- **d030_kl50_uniform** (cuda:0): KL 50% sparsity + uniform per-layer allocation 评测（benchmark_eval.py 跑 wikitext2+arc+wino+mmlu+gsm8k）。验证 50% 高 sparsity 下 uniform 是否仍能修复知识类任务。

已完成关键结论：
- D030 30% uniform: MMLU +12pp 修复，GSM8K 无变化 → Narrative A (allocation-sensitive vs invariant) 已被 Director 选定
- 50% sparsity downstream collapse 跨架构 universal（Llama + Mistral 都崩）
- PPL-downstream disconnect 已三重验证，有文献支撑但无人在 KL+contextual sparsity 下系统研究
- bench_mistral_kl50_s42 (PENDING/killed): 被 BCE v3 插队，待 BCE 完成后重跑

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **d030_kl50_uniform cron 回调** → 分析结果：MMLU 是否仍有修复效果？GSM8K 是否仍为 0？→ 确认/修正 Narrative A。若 MMLU 仍有 +Xpp → Narrative A 成立扩展到 50%；若 MMLU 也崩 → 50% 是断崖点，需更新 framing
- **bce_isocompute_v3_s42 cron 回调** → 提取 BCE 2700 步 TEAL PPL，与 KL 1000 步 (PPL 12.00) iso-compute 对比。BCE PPL >> 12.00 → Reviewer Concern #2 defense 成立（KL 优势来自 loss 本身非计算量）；BCE PPL ≈ 12.00 → 需重新评估 claim
- **两个实验都完成后** → cuda:0/1 空闲 → 立即提交 bench_mistral_kl50_s42（D029 统一 few-shot），以及 PREPARING 队列中优先级最高的实验
- **所有 downstream eval 完成** → 更新 paper_framing.md Claim 3，根据 uniform 结果决定最终 narrative

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**:
- D028: 50% downstream collapse 跨架构 universal
- D030: collapse 有两个正交因素——per-layer 不均匀（影响知识类）+ sparsity level 本身（影响推理类）
- D031: BCE iso-compute 是 Reviewer Concern #2 的最后一块拼图
- D032: Mistral PPL 数据污染警告，暂停引用

**易错点**:
- 不要把 d030_kl50_uniform 的 benchmark_eval.py 结果与旧 lm_eval 0-shot 结果直接对比——few-shot 设置不同（D029）
- PREPARING 状态的 7 个实验全部 conclusion=killed，它们是历史遗留，不要试图恢复
- submit_training_job 的命令必须自带环境激活（`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`），不能假设 .venv
- bce_isocompute_v3 用了 nohup（因 v1/v2 被 tmux 杀死），确认它的 cron 监控的是 wrapper_pid=205597
- Worker 池当前为 0，接手后应立即评估是否需要派 Worker 做文献/分析/代码准备等非 GPU 任务

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\"}, \"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Training Summary\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\n\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\n\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). 482.65, \"spon_correction_norm\": 76.2, \"interaction_norm\": 147.74, \"num_samples\": 50, \"seq_len\": 2048, \"conclusion_type\": \"weak_subsumption\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"ste_baseline_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\n\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## GSM8K 生成质量对比：Dense vs KL Sparse 50% (10 samples)\\\\n\\\\n### 结论：推理能力根本性丧失（模式 A），非仅数字算错\\\\n\\\\n| 维度 | Dense | Sparse 50% |\\\\n|------|-------|------------|\\\\n| 推理连贯性 | 多步推理，逻辑清晰 | 1-3 句后进入无限重复循环 |\\\\n| 正确答案 | 多数正确 | 0/10 |\\\\n| 重复退化 | 无 | 10/10 全部出现 |\\\\n| 拼写质量 | 正常 | 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\\\n\\\\n### 退化模式\\\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\\\\\"First, we need to know...\\\\\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\\\n\\\\n### 对论文的意义\\\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\\\\\"自信地\\\\\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\\\"}, \\\"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"teal_sparse_note\\\": \\\"17.91 = predictor-based TEAL (teal_global_masks: predictor-gated magnitude scoring + global topk @70%). vanilla TEAL @70% = 100.11. 代码来源: quick_sp70.py L10 imports 32, \\\"per_layer_sparsity_range\\\": [0.508, 0.876]}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\\\n\\\\n### Training Summary\\\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\\\n\\\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\\\n\\\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). Result: 136K → 17.91.\\\"}, \\\"training_free_baselines\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T18:31:40.470Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T23:53:32.400Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"dense_ppl\\\": 6.12, \\\"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_30pct\\\": 10.97, \\\"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_50pct\\\": 23.31, \\\"vanilla_teal_global_ppl_70pct\\\": 100.11, \\\"wina_global_ppl_30pct\\\": 11.45, \\\"wina_global_ppl_50pct\\\": 27.93, \\\"wina_global_ppl_70pct\\\": 137.24, \\\"uniform_50pct_ppl\\\": 23.9, \\\"teal_greedy_ppl\\\": 33.47, \\\"vanilla_teal_global_ppl\\\": 23.31, \\\"wina_global_ppl\\\": 27.93, \\\"rsparse_global_ppl\\\": 151.2}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"2026-05-16 全部用 D017 bugfix (per-layer mean normalization) 后代码统一重跑确认。三个 sparsity level 全部物理一致：30% < 50% < 70%。TEAL: 10.97 < 23.31 < 100.11, WINA: 11.45 < 27.93 < 137.24。旧 70% TEAL=17.91 是 predictor-based TEAL (非 vanilla TEAL)，不可直接比较。代码确认 _global_topk_masks 包含 per-layer mean normalization (s_f / mu)。环境: HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1, setup_env.sh sourced。\\\"}, \\\"lm_eval_dense\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T22:39:59.634Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T23:46:22.204Z\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"arc_challenge_acc_norm\\\": 0.5819, \\\"winogrande_acc\\\": 0.7814, \\\"mmlu_acc\\\": 0.6532, \\\"gsm8k_exact_match\\\": 0.5004, \\\"eval_tasks\\\": [\\\"arc_challenge\\\", \\\"winogrande\\\", \\\"mmlu\\\", \\\"gsm8k\\\"], \\\"model\\\": \\\"llama-3.1-8b\\\", \\\"condition\\\": \\\"dense\\\", \\\"server\\\": \\\"westd-34887\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"kl_sparsity30_s42_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T20:30:30.528Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T21:14:59.706Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"KL distillation @ 30% sparsity (mild pruning). {\\\"linearity_ratio\\\": 0.2644, \\\"linearity_ratio_std\\\": 0.0073, \\\"magnitude_ratio\\\": 0.1578, \\\"magnitude_ratio_std\\\": 0.0088, \\\"cosine_similarity\\\": 0.1962, \\\"cosine_similarity_std\\\": 0.0268, \\\"predictor_effect_norm\\\": 482.65, \\\"spon_correction_norm\\\": 76.2, \\\"interaction_norm\\\": 147.74, \\\"num_samples\\\": 50, \\\"seq_len\\\": 2048, \\\"conclusion_type\\\": \\\"weak_subsumption\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\\\n\\\\n### 核心指标\\\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\\\n\\\\n### Effect magnitudes\\\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n\\\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\\\n\\\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\\\\\"marginal complement\\\\\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\\\\\"subsumption\\\\\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\\\n\\\\n### 产出文件\\\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\\\"}, \\\"ste_baseline_s42\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T18:35:42.177Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T20:03:55.571Z\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"STE baseline 代码实现完成 (Worker worker_code_ste_baseline_20260515T183635)。修改：src/predictor.py 新增 STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\\\n\\\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\\\n\\\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\\\n\\\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\\\n\\\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\\\n\\\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\\\n\\\\n## 根因分析\\\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\\\n\\\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Iso-compute BCE: 2700 步匹配 KL 1000 步的总 FLOPs (ratio=2.71x, 实测 profile_flops.py: KL 0.549s/step vs BCE 0.202s/step @BS=1,SL=512)。\\\\\\\\nReviewer Concern #2 defense: 验证 KL 优势来自 loss 函数本身而非计算量差异。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## FLOPs 计算依据\\\\\\\\n- KL 每步: dense fwd (no grad) + sparse fwd (grad) + backward w/ gradient checkpointing = 0.549s\\\\\\\\n- BCE 每步: dense fwd (no grad) + predictor fwd/bwd (~0.9% model FLOPs) = 0.202s\\\\\\\\n- Ratio: 2.71x → 1000 KL steps ≈ 2700 BCE steps\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 失败记录 [2026-05-15]\\\\\\\\n进程在 Step 500/2700 后静默退出（PID 120879）。日志仅 17 行，最后记录 Step 500: 退化（crabs→crbs, skein→skeen）|\\n\\n### 退化模式\\nSparse 模型能生成与问题相关的开头（如 \\\"First, we need to know...\\\"），但随即进入同一句话的无限重复（15+ 次）。Sample 7 中正确算出 9×2=18，但无法继续推理。\\n\\n### 对论文的意义\\n50% sparsity 摧毁了深层推理能力。PPL-downstream disconnect 的机制：PPL 衡量 next-token 平均质量，repetition loop 的每个重复 token 仍有较高概率（模型\\\"自信地\\\"重复），但推理链断裂。\"}, \"kl_sparsity70_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Training Analysis (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Training Summary\\n- 1000/1000 steps completed, EXIT_CODE=0\\n- Sparsity converged to ~0.688 (target 0.70, within 0.65-0.75 success range)\\n- Lambda hit cap 1000 by step 10, tau annealed 0.999 → 0.100\\n- KL loss: 1.18 → 0.61\\n\\n### PPL Evaluation Results\\n- true_dense_ppl: 6.12\\n- predictor_hard_mask_ppl: **13.84** (per-token adaptive mask)\\n- teal_sparse_ppl: **17.91** (static global topk, FIXED via D017)\\n- model_level_sparsity: 0.699\\n\\n### D017 Bug Fix (2026-05-15)\\nOriginal teal_sparse_ppl=136,069 was caused by using raw sigmoid scores for global topk mask selection. Sigmoid probabilities lack magnitude information and are poor proxies for static neuron importance. Fix: score neurons by mean(|intermediate| * predictor_hard_mask) — activation magnitude weighted by predictor decisions. Also added per-layer mean normalization (from _global_topk_masks). 482.65, \"spon_correction_norm\": 76.2, \"interaction_norm\": 147.74, \"num_samples\": 50, \"seq_len\": 2048, \"conclusion_type\": \"weak_subsumption\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"ste_baseline_s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\n\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", Mistral KL 50% predictor checkpoint 的准确路径（`ls /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/ | grep mistral`）\n   c. 确认 Mistral 模型路径存在: `ls /root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`\n   d. 查看 d030_kl50_uniform 或 d030_kl30_uniform 的启动命令作为参考（查 bash history 或 tmux list 或日志开头）\n   e. 检查 benchmark_eval.py 需要哪些离线数据（HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 是否需要，数据是否已下载）\n\n2. 构造完整的提交命令（bash -c 包裹），格式：\n```\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu && HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 python benchmark_eval.py --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/ --predictor_path &lt;CHECKPOINT_PATH> --sparsity 0.5 --output_dir results/bench_mistral_kl50_s42 [其他必要参数]\"\n```\n\n3. **只报告命令，不要执行它。** 把完整命令和你找到的所有路径信息写到产出里。\n\n### 注意\n- GPU 是 cuda:0，CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 由框架自动注入，脚本内用 --gpu 0\n- 确认是否需要 --allocation uniform 或 global（这个实验用默认 global top-k，不是 uniform）\n- 确认 few-shot 设置参数名\n",
